Turn 66 · 我现在要动态调参，现在的问题是小车喜欢横着走，但实际上不能，应该转弯后车头对着路径走，应该在命令行输入什么
completed
User 2026-05-05T15:08:51.058Z

我现在要动态调参，现在的问题是小车喜欢横着走，但实际上不能，应该转弯后车头对着路径走，应该在命令行输入什么

Assistant 2026-05-05T15:09:06.897Z

你现在这个问题优先调“车头跟路径”和“禁止横切”。直接一条一行输入：

ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.max_vel_theta 1.4
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.acc_lim_theta 1.4
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_forward_drive 80.0
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_turning_radius 100.0
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_nh 1500.0
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_viapoint 8.0
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.global_plan_viapoint_sep 0.3
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.allow_init_with_backwards_motion false

同步 smoother：

ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_velocity "[2.2, 0.0, 1.4]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother min_velocity "[-0.5, 0.0, -1.4]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_accel "[2.2, 0.0, 1.4]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_decel "[-2.2, 0.0, -1.4]"

如果还是横着切弯，就降低入弯线速度：

ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.max_vel_x 1.8
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.acc_lim_x 1.8
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_velocity "[1.8, 0.0, 1.4]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_accel "[1.8, 0.0, 1.4]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_decel "[-1.8, 0.0, -1.4]"

如果开始自转或抖动，把角速度降回：

ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.max_vel_theta 1.2
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.acc_lim_theta 1.2
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_velocity "[2.2, 0.0, 1.2]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_accel "[2.2, 0.0, 1.2]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_decel "[-2.2, 0.0, -1.2]"

确认 y 方向没有被打开：

ros2 param get /controller_server FollowPath.max_vel_y
ros2 param get /controller_server FollowPath.acc_lim_y
ros2 param get /velocity_smoother max_velocity

预期第二个值都是 0.0。

Assistant 2026-05-05T15:15:57.014Z

先只改这一组，目标是：禁止横向速度、增强非全向约束、让车头更愿意贴着全局路径方向走。

在仿真运行时，新开一个终端，先 source：

source /opt/ros/humble/setup.bash
source ~/venom_ws/install/setup.bash
source ~/venom_ws/src/venom_vnv/simulation/venom_nav_simulation/install/setup.bash

然后逐行执行：

ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.max_vel_y 0.0
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.acc_lim_y 0.0

ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_nh 1500.0
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_forward_drive 80.0
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_turning_radius 80.0

ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.global_plan_overwrite_orientation true
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.allow_init_with_backwards_motion false

ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_velocity "[2.0, 0.0, 1.2]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother min_velocity "[-0.3, 0.0, -1.2]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_accel "[2.0, 0.0, 1.2]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_decel "[-2.0, 0.0, -1.2]"

然后确认参数已经生效：

ros2 param get /controller_server FollowPath.max_vel_y
ros2 param get /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_nh
ros2 param get /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_forward_drive
ros2 param get /controller_server FollowPath.global_plan_overwrite_orientation
ros2 param get /velocity_smoother max_velocity

这次先不要同时改太多。你在 RViz 里重新给一个目标点，看现象：

如果还是横着切弯，下一步再把这两个继续加大：

ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_nh 2000.0
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_turning_radius 120.0

如果它开始原地扭、不往前走，就把 global_plan_overwrite_orientation 改回 false。

Commentary 2026-05-05T15:20:51.334Z

我查到两点对这次很关键：TEB 官方示例里非全向车 max_vel_y/acc_lim_y 必须为 0；类车模型需要设置合理 min_turning_radius，但权重过大或角速度/加速度太低会让局部优化没有可行解，表现就是不动或来回抖。我会把现在过强的约束降到可动范围，同时保留禁止横移。

Reasoning 2026-05-05T15:22:12.782Z

Reasoning captured privately.

Assistant 2026-05-05T15:22:21.289Z

已直接改文件：nav2_params_sim.yaml

这次不是继续把约束加硬，而是按成熟 TEB 思路改成“可动的非全向约束”：

  • max_vel_y: 0.0、acc_lim_y: 0.0 保持禁止横移。
  • weight_kinematics_nh 从 1000 降到 100，避免优化器被压死导致车不动。
  • weight_kinematics_turning_radius 从 60 降到 5，先保证可行轨迹。
  • min_turning_radius 从 0.8 降到 0.45，避免转弯半径过大导致局部规划找不到路。
  • max_vel_theta 提到 2.0，acc_lim_theta 提到 2.5，让车头能更快追上路径方向。
  • velocity_smoother 同步改成 [2.0, 0.0, 2.0] / [2.0, 0.0, 2.5]，避免后级限幅和 TEB 不一致。

我也把这次修改记到：change_log.md

我参考的是 TEB 官方仓库和配置说明思路：TEB 是同时优化时间、避障和运动学约束的局部规划器；类车/Ackermann 场景要用 min_turning_radius、wheelbase、非全向约束等参数，但这些约束不能一次拉太大，否则容易无可行解。来源：
https://github.com/rst-tu-dortmund/teb_local_planner
https://deepwiki.com/rst-tu-dortmund/teb_local_planner/5.3-common-use-cases

重新启动仿真后测试，不要用刚才命令行动态参数覆盖：

cd ~/venom_ws/src/venom_vnv/simulation/venom_nav_simulation
source /opt/ros/humble/setup.bash
source ~/venom_ws/install/setup.bash
source install/setup.bash

ros2 launch rm_nav_bringup bringup_sim.launch.py \
  world:=RMUL \
  mode:=nav \
  lio:=pointlio \
  localization:=slam_toolbox \
  lio_rviz:=False \
  nav_rviz:=True

如果这版能动但仍有轻微斜切，下一步只加 weight_kinematics_nh，不要再同时改一堆参数。

#include "fake_vel_transform/fake_vel_transform.hpp"

#include <tf2/utils.h>

#include <rclcpp/logging.hpp>
#include <rclcpp/qos.hpp>
#include <rclcpp/utilities.hpp>

namespace fake_vel_transform
{
const std::string CMD_VEL_TOPIC = "/cmd_vel";
const std::string AFTER_TF_CMD_VEL = "/cmd_vel_chassis";
const std::string TRAJECTORY_TOPIC = "/local_plan";
const int TF_PUBLISH_FREQUENCY = 20;  // base_link to base_link_fake. Frequency in Hz.

FakeVelTransform::FakeVelTransform(const rclcpp::NodeOptions & options)
: Node("fake_vel_transform", options)
{
  RCLCPP_INFO(get_logger(), "Start FakeVelTransform!");

  // Declare and get the spin speed parameter
  this->declare_parameter<float>("spin_speed", -6.0);
  this->get_parameter("spin_speed", spin_speed_);

  // TF broadcaster
  tf_broadcaster_ = std::make_unique<tf2_ros::TransformBroadcaster>(*this);
  tf2_buffer_ = std::make_unique<tf2_ros::Buffer>(this->get_clock());
  tf2_listener_ = std::make_shared<tf2_ros::TransformListener>(*tf2_buffer_);

  // Create Publisher and Subscriber
  cmd_vel_sub_ = this->create_subscription<geometry_msgs::msg::Twist>(
    CMD_VEL_TOPIC, 1, std::bind(&FakeVelTransform::cmdVelCallback, this, std::placeholders::_1));
  cmd_vel_chassis_pub_ = this->create_publisher<geometry_msgs::msg::Twist>(
    AFTER_TF_CMD_VEL, rclcpp::QoS(rclcpp::KeepLast(1)));
  local_pose_sub_ = this->create_subscription<nav_msgs::msg::Path>(
    TRAJECTORY_TOPIC, 1,
    std::bind(&FakeVelTransform::localPoseCallback, this, std::placeholders::_1));

  // Create a timer to publish the transform regularly
  tf_timer_ = this->create_wall_timer(
    std::chrono::milliseconds(1000 / TF_PUBLISH_FREQUENCY),
    std::bind(&FakeVelTransform::publishTransform, this));
}

// Get the local pose from planner
void FakeVelTransform::localPoseCallback(const nav_msgs::msg::Path::SharedPtr msg)
{
  if (!msg || msg->poses.empty()) {
    RCLCPP_WARN(get_logger(), "Received empty or invalid PoseArray message");
    return;
  }

  // Choose the pose based on the size of the poses array
  size_t index = std::min(msg->poses.size() / 4, msg->poses.size() - 1);
  const geometry_msgs::msg::Pose & selected_pose = msg->poses[index].pose;

  // Update current angle based on the difference between teb_angle and base_link_angle_
  double teb_angle = tf2::getYaw(selected_pose.orientation);
  current_angle_ = teb_angle - base_link_angle_;
}

// Transform the velocity from base_link to base_link_fake
void FakeVelTransform::cmdVelCallback(const geometry_msgs::msg::Twist::SharedPtr msg)
{
  try {
    geometry_msgs::msg::TransformStamped transform_stamped;
    transform_stamped = tf2_buffer_->lookupTransform("odom", "base_link", tf2::TimePointZero);
    base_link_angle_ = tf2::getYaw(transform_stamped.transform.rotation);

    geometry_msgs::msg::Twist aft_tf_vel;
    aft_tf_vel.angular.z = msg->angular.z + spin_speed_;
    aft_tf_vel.linear.x = msg->linear.x;
    aft_tf_vel.linear.y = 0.0;

    cmd_vel_chassis_pub_->publish(aft_tf_vel);
  } catch (tf2::TransformException & ex) {
    RCLCPP_INFO(this->get_logger(), "Could not transform odom to base_link: %s", ex.what());
  }
}

// Publish transform from base_link to base_link_fake
void FakeVelTransform::publishTransform()
{
  geometry_msgs::msg::TransformStamped t;
  t.header.stamp = get_clock()->now();
  t.header.frame_id = "base_link";
  t.child_frame_id = "base_link_fake";
  tf2::Quaternion q;
  q.setRPY(0, 0, current_angle_);
  t.transform.rotation = tf2::toMsg(q);
  tf_broadcaster_->sendTransform(t);
}

}  // namespace fake_vel_transform

#include "rclcpp_components/register_node_macro.hpp"

// Register the component with class_loader.
// This acts as a sort of entry point, allowing the component to be discoverable when its library
// is being loaded into a running process.
RCLCPP_COMPONENTS_REGISTER_NODE(fake_vel_transform::FakeVelTransform)

Commentary 2026-05-05T15:25:29.311Z

现在可以判断原因了：base_link_fake 让 Nav2 以“路径朝向”作为机器人朝向，但 fake_vel_transform 现在把横向速度强行清零后，没有再把真实 base_link 车头转过去。所以 TEB 以为车头已经对着路径，实际车身一直保持原朝向。我要改 fake_vel_transform，让它根据 base_link_fake 和真实 base_link 的角度误差主动输出角速度。

Commentary 2026-05-05T15:25:49.787Z

我会改的是控制链路，不是再堆 TEB 权重：当 base_link_fake 的期望朝向和真实 base_link 不一致时，fake_vel_transform 主动给 /cmd_vel_chassis.angular.z 加一个“追车头”的角速度，并在偏差大时降低前进速度。这样车不是横着平移，而是先把车头转向路径再走。

Reasoning 2026-05-05T15:27:28.672Z

Reasoning captured privately.

Assistant 2026-05-05T15:27:34.843Z

我确认了，这个问题的根因不是单纯 TEB 参数：你现在的仿真链路用 base_link_fake 让 Nav2 以“路径方向”作为车头方向，但之前 fake_vel_transform 把横向速度清零后，没有主动让真实 base_link 转向，所以表现就是小车一直一个朝向往前滑。

我已经改了并编译通过：

  • fake_vel_transform.cpp：根据路径期望朝向和真实车头朝向的误差，给 /cmd_vel_chassis.angular.z 增加角速度修正。
  • bringup_sim.launch.py：新增 heading_kp、max_heading_angular_vel、heading_slowdown_angle。
  • 已记录到 change_log.md。
  • colcon build --symlink-install --packages-select fake_vel_transform 已成功。

现在重新启动仿真：

cd ~/venom_ws/src/venom_vnv/simulation/venom_nav_simulation
source /opt/ros/humble/setup.bash
source ~/venom_ws/install/setup.bash
source install/setup.bash

ros2 launch rm_nav_bringup bringup_sim.launch.py \
  world:=RMUL \
  mode:=nav \
  lio:=pointlio \
  localization:=slam_toolbox \
  lio_rviz:=False \
  nav_rviz:=True

测试时另开终端看底盘角速度有没有输出：

ros2 topic echo /cmd_vel_chassis --field angular.z

如果转弯时这里有非零值，说明车头转向命令已经发到底盘；如果 Gazebo 里仍然不转，那就不是 TEB 了，而是 Gazebo planar move 插件或模型朝向的问题。

void TebVisualization::publishLocalPlan(const std::vector<geometry_msgs::msg::PoseStamped>& local_plan) const
{
  if ( printErrorWhenNotInitialized() )
    return;
  publishPlan(local_plan, local_plan_pub_.get());
}

void TebVisualization::publishLocalPlanAndPoses(const TimedElasticBand& teb) const
{
  if ( printErrorWhenNotInitialized() )
    return;
  
    // create path msg
    nav_msgs::msg::Path teb_path;
    teb_path.header.frame_id = cfg_->map_frame;
    teb_path.header.stamp = nh_->now();
    
    // create pose_array (along trajectory)
    geometry_msgs::msg::PoseArray teb_poses;
    teb_poses.header.frame_id = teb_path.header.frame_id;
    teb_poses.header.stamp = teb_path.header.stamp;
    
    // fill path msgs with teb configurations
    for (int i=0; i < teb.sizePoses(); i++)
    {
      geometry_msgs::msg::PoseStamped pose;

      pose.header.frame_id = teb_path.header.frame_id;
      pose.header.stamp = teb_path.header.stamp;
      teb.Pose(i).toPoseMsg(pose.pose);
      pose.pose.position.z = cfg_->hcp.visualize_with_time_as_z_axis_scale*teb.getSumOfTimeDiffsUpToIdx(i);
      teb_path.poses.push_back(pose);
      teb_poses.poses.push_back(pose.pose);
    }
    local_plan_pub_->publish(teb_path);
    teb_poses_pub_->publish(teb_poses);
}

Commentary 2026-05-05T15:30:14.751Z

定位到了：TEB 的 local_plan 是在 controller_server 节点下用相对话题发布的，所以实际话题应是 /controller_server/local_plan；fake_vel_transform 现在订阅的是根话题 /local_plan，大概率收不到任何路径。我改成默认订阅 /controller_server/local_plan，再编译。

Assistant 2026-05-05T15:31:25.662Z

原因找到了：fake_vel_transform 订阅错了局部路径话题。

TEB 的局部路径不是发到 /local_plan，而是 controller_server 节点下的相对话题，所以实际是：

/controller_server/local_plan

之前 fake_vel_transform 收不到路径，current_angle_ 一直是 0，所以我加的车头修正也一直算出 0，最终 /cmd_vel_chassis.angular.z 显示为 0。

我已经改好并重新编译成功：

  • fake_vel_transform.cpp：默认订阅 /controller_server/local_plan
  • bringup_sim.launch.py：显式传入 trajectory_topic
  • 日志已写入 change_log.md

现在重启仿真，然后给目标点。转弯时再看：

ros2 topic echo /cmd_vel_chassis --field angular.z

同时也可以确认路径话题确实有数据：

ros2 topic hz /controller_server/local_plan

如果 /controller_server/local_plan 有频率，且 /cmd_vel_chassis.angular.z 转弯时变成非零，说明车头修正链路已经通了。
